script.k21abs	ok
script.k21abs.text
  |% Create target build options object, set build properties and build.
  |buildOpts = compiler.build.StandaloneApplicationOptions("<W>\p\p\k21abs\k21_hello.m");
  |buildOpts.AdditionalFiles = ["<MATLABROOT>\toolbox\compiler\Contents.m", "C:\forward\slash.txt"];
  |buildOpts.AutoDetectDataFiles = true;
  |buildOpts.OutputDir = "<W>\p\p\k21abs\k21abs\for_testing";
  |buildOpts.Verbose = true;
  |buildOpts.EmbedArchive = true;
  |buildOpts.ExecutableName = "k21abs";
  |buildOpts.ExecutableVersion = "1.0";
  |buildOpts.TreatInputsAsNumeric = false;
  |buildResult = compiler.build.standaloneWindowsApplication(buildOpts);
  |
  |
  |% Create package options object, set package properties and package.
  |packageOpts = compiler.package.InstallerOptions(buildResult);
  |packageOpts.ApplicationName = "k21abs";
  |packageOpts.DefaultInstallationDir = "%ProgramFiles%\k21abs\";
  |packageOpts.InstallerName = "MyAppInstaller_web";
  |packageOpts.OutputDir = "<W>\p\p\k21abs\k21abs\for_redistribution";
  |packageOpts.Verbose = true;
  |packageOpts.Version = "1.0";
  |compiler.package.installer(buildResult, "Options", packageOpts);

  |projectRoot = "<W>\p\p\k21std";
  |
  |% Create target build options object, set build properties and build.
  |buildOpts = compiler.build.StandaloneApplicationOptions(fullfile(projectRoot, "k21_hello.m"));
  |buildOpts.AdditionalFiles = [fullfile(projectRoot, "k21_a.txt"), fullfile(projectRoot, "k21_b.txt"), fullfile(projectRoot, "k21_data.mat"), fullfile(projectRoot, "k21_note.txt")];
  |buildOpts.AutoDetectDataFiles = true;
  |buildOpts.OutputDir = fullfile(projectRoot, "k21std", "for_testing");
  |buildOpts.ObfuscateArchive = false;
  |buildOpts.Verbose = true;
  |helpTextFile = tempname;
  |fileID = fopen(helpTextFile,'w');
  |ensureFileCloseObject = onCleanup(@()fclose(fileID));
  |fprintf(fileID, "Usage: k21std name\nPrints hello.");
  |ensureFileDeleteObject = onCleanup(@()delete(helpTextFile));
  |buildOpts.CustomHelpTextFile = helpTextFile;
  |buildOpts.EmbedArchive = false;
  |buildOpts.ExecutableIcon = fullfile(projectRoot, "k21_icon.png");
  |buildOpts.ExecutableName = "k21std";
  |buildOpts.ExecutableSplashScreen = fullfile(projectRoot, "k21_splash.png");
  |buildOpts.ExecutableVersion = "2.3.1";
  |buildOpts.TreatInputsAsNumeric = true;
  |buildResult = compiler.build.standaloneApplication(buildOpts);
  |
  |
  |% Create package options object, set package properties and package.
  |packageOpts = compiler.package.InstallerOptions(buildResult);
  |packageOpts.ApplicationName = "k21std";
  |packageOpts.AuthorName = "Ada Lovelace";
  |packageOpts.AuthorEmail = "ada@example.com";
  |packageOpts.AuthorCompany = "Analytical Engines";
  |packageOpts.DefaultInstallationDir = "/Analytical Engines/k21std/";
  |packageOpts.Description = "Prints a greeting." + newline + "Two lines.";
  |packageOpts.InstallerIcon = fullfile(projectRoot, "k21_icon.png");
  |packageOpts.InstallerLogo = fullfile(projectRoot, "k21_logo.png");
  |packageOpts.InstallerName = "MyAppInstaller_web";
  |packageOpts.InstallerSplash = fullfile(projectRoot, "k21_splash.png");
  |packageOpts.InstallationNotes = "Notes line 1" + newline + "Notes line 2";
  |packageOpts.OutputDir = fullfile(projectRoot, "k21std", "for_redistribution");
  |packageOpts.Summary = "A greeting";
  |packageOpts.Verbose = true;
  |packageOpts.Version = "2.3.1";
  |compiler.package.installer(buildResult, "Options", packageOpts);
